grep -q \"checkpoint_fresh=1\" \"$LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected 'checkpoint_fresh=1' in log\"\n  cat \"$LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: pending + fresh checkpoint → no ALERT\"\n\n# --- TC6: STRICT mode + pending + stale → exit 2 ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sessions/2026-05-05-session-pending.md\" <<'EOF'\n# AskUserQuestion fired. SCOPE-tier deferral surfaced.\nEOF\necho \"# Stale checkpoint\" > \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\ntouch -d \"3 hours ago\" \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSTDERR6=\"$TEMPDIR/tc6.stderr\"\nRC6=0\nSTOCKFORGE_HANDOFF_HARDBLOCK=1 CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>\"$STDERR6\" || RC6=$?\nif [ \"$RC6\" != \"2\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: expected exit 2 (STRICT mode); got $RC6\"\n  cat \"$STDERR6\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"HARD-BLOCK\" \"$HOOK_LOG\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: expected 'HARD-BLOCK' marker in $HOOK_LOG\"\n  cat \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: STRICT mode + pending + stale → exit 2\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"pre-clear-handoff-guard.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"533fba62-0cba-49b8-9fd4-73c60b4f4f78","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d6a934c6-2410-424f-817b-82d8b866edc2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Render decision-class thresholds (CHARTER/SCOPE/ARCH/IMPL).\n# Render top-10 most recent events from events.tsv. Atomic write (tmp + mv);\n# idempotent.\n#\n# Test strategy: hook computes ROOT_DIR via $(dirname $0)/../.. so we copy\n# hook to TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/ (ROOT_DIR resolves to TEMPDIR), stage\n# state/events/weights fixtures, invoke hook, verify _index.md content.\n#\n# Per L-S62-1: avoid `yes | head` under pipefail; use `seq | printf` for\n# deterministic line counts.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — score=95 → tier \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\"\n#   TC2 — score=20 → tier \"MUST-GRILL\"\n#   TC3 — 5 categories at 5 different tiers → all 5 rows + correct tier text\n#   TC4 — events.tsv with 12 entries → top-10 only in output\n#   TC5 — thresholds rendered from weights.yaml (CHARTER 99 / SCOPE 90 / IMPL 50)\n#   TC6 — idempotent: 2nd run produces same _index.md modulo RENDER_TS line\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\n# Copy hook to TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/ so $(dirname $0)/../.. resolves to TEMPDIR\nHOOK_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$HOOK_DIR\"\ncp \"$HOOK\" \"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nchmod +x \"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nHOOK_COPY=\"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n\nSYNC_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\nINDEX=\"$SYNC_DIR/_index.md\"\n\nstage_weights() {\n  mkdir -p \"$SYNC_DIR\"\n  cat > \"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\" <<'EOF'\n# test fixture\nthreshold_CHARTER: 99\nthreshold_SCOPE: 90\nthreshold_ARCH: 80\nthreshold_IMPL: 50\ntier_HIGH: 90\ntier_MED_HIGH: 70\ntier_MED: 50\ntier_MED_LOW: 30\ntier_MUST_GRILL: 0\nEOF\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  stage_weights\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  bash \"$HOOK_COPY\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\n# --- TC1: score=95 → HIGH-CONFIDENCE tier ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\n2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\tLANGUAGE\tdecision_correctness\t0.5\tD-001\tsrc-1\tr1\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif [ ! -f \"$INDEX\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: $INDEX not produced\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected 'HIGH-CONFIDENCE' for score=95\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: score=95 → HIGH-CONFIDENCE\"\n\n# --- TC2: score=20 → MUST-GRILL tier ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nSCOPE\t20\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t3\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"MUST-GRILL\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'MUST-GRILL' for score=20\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: score=20 → MUST-GRILL\"\n\n# --- TC3: 5 categories at 5 different tier levels ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nDOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\t75\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nDESIGN_THINKING\t55\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nSCOPE\t35\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t2\nDECISION_ROUTING\t15\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t5\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\n# Each tier should appear once via distinct score-range markers\nfor tier_marker in \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\" \"MED-HIGH\" \"0.50-0.69\" \"MED-LOW\" \"MUST-GRILL\"; do\n  if ! grep -qF \"$tier_marker\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL TC3: expected tier marker '$tier_marker' in output\"\n    cat \"$INDEX\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n# All 5 categories should appear as table rows\nfor cat_name in LANGUAGE DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS DESIGN_THINKING SCOPE DECISION_ROUTING; do\n  if ! grep -qF \"| $cat_name |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL TC3: expected category $cat_name row\"\n    cat \"$INDEX\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\necho \"PASS TC3: 5 categories at 5 tier levels rendered\"\n\n# --- TC4: events.tsv with 12 entries → top-10 only ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t50\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\n{\n  printf \"ts\\tcategory\\tevent_type\\tdelta\\tdecision_id\\tsource_evidence\\treason\\n\"\n  # Generate 12 events deterministically (per L-S62-1: no `yes|head`; seq+printf instead)\n  for i in $(seq 1 12); do\n    printf \"2026-05-05T10:%02d:00Z\\tLANGUAGE\\tdecision_correctness\\t0.5\\tD-%03d\\tsrc-%d\\tevent-%02d\\n\" \"$i\" \"$i\" \"$i\" \"$i\"\n  done\n} > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\nrun_hook\nEVENT_COUNT=$(grep -c \"^| 2026-05-05T10:\" \"$INDEX\" || echo 0)\nif [ \"$EVENT_COUNT\" != \"10\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected 10 event rows (top-10); got $EVENT_COUNT\"\n  grep \"^| 2026-05-05T10:\" \"$INDEX\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n# events 03-12 should appear (last 10 from tail -n 10), events 01-02 should NOT\nif grep -q \"event-01\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: oldest event-01 should be tail-cut\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"event-12\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: newest event-12 should be present\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: 12 events → top-10 rendered (oldest 2 cut)\"\n\n# --- TC5: thresholds rendered from weights.yaml ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t50\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -qF \"| CHARTER | 99 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| CHARTER | 99 |' threshold row\"\n  grep \"CHARTER\" \"$INDEX\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| SCOPE | 90 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| SCOPE | 90 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| ARCH | 80 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| ARCH | 80 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| IMPL | 50 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| IMPL | 50 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: thresholds rendered from weights.yaml\"\n\n# --- TC6: idempotent — 2nd run produces same _index.md modulo RENDER_TS ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\ncp \"$INDEX\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.md\"\nrun_hook\n# Filter out the variable RENDER_TS line (\"> **Last rendered**: ...\")\ngrep -v \"Last rendered\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.md\" > \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\"\ngrep -v \"Last rendered\" \"$INDEX\"           > \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\"\nif ! diff -q \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: idempotency violated outside RENDER_TS line\"\n  diff \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"Confidence Score Index\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: 2nd run output missing header\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: idempotent — 2nd run identical modulo RENDER_TS\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"sync-tracker-render.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":16112,"cache_read_input_tokens":114673,"output_tokens":8763,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":8763,"cache_read_input_tokens":114673,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CajjwYgpNQYgg8t7nRwJ7","type":"assistant","uuid":"fbdf952e-b183-4273-9d20-88965926f9bf","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:32:49.649Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d6a934c6-2410-424f-817b-82d8b866edc2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Atomic write (tmp + mv);\n# idempotent.\n#\n# Test strategy: hook computes ROOT_DIR via $(dirname $0)/../.. so we copy\n# hook to TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/ (ROOT_DIR resolves to TEMPDIR), stage\n# state/events/weights fixtures, invoke hook, verify _index.md content.\n#\n# Per L-S62-1: avoid `yes | head` under pipefail; use `seq | printf` for\n# deterministic line counts.\n#\n# 6 test cases:\n#   TC1 — score=95 → tier \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\"\n#   TC2 — score=20 → tier \"MUST-GRILL\"\n#   TC3 — 5 categories at 5 different tiers → all 5 rows + correct tier text\n#   TC4 — events.tsv with 12 entries → top-10 only in output\n#   TC5 — thresholds rendered from weights.yaml (CHARTER 99 / SCOPE 90 / IMPL 50)\n#   TC6 — idempotent: 2nd run produces same _index.md modulo RENDER_TS line\n#\n# Exit 0 = all assertions pass. Exit 1 = any assertion fail.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n[ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ] && { echo \"FAIL: hook script not found at $HOOK\"; exit 1; }\n\nTEMPDIR=$(mktemp -d)\ntrap \"rm -rf $TEMPDIR\" EXIT\n\n# Copy hook to TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks/ so $(dirname $0)/../.. resolves to TEMPDIR\nHOOK_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/scripts/hooks\"\nmkdir -p \"$HOOK_DIR\"\ncp \"$HOOK\" \"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nchmod +x \"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\nHOOK_COPY=\"$HOOK_DIR/sync-tracker-render.sh\"\n\nSYNC_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker\"\nINDEX=\"$SYNC_DIR/_index.md\"\n\nstage_weights() {\n  mkdir -p \"$SYNC_DIR\"\n  cat > \"$SYNC_DIR/weights.yaml\" <<'EOF'\n# test fixture\nthreshold_CHARTER: 99\nthreshold_SCOPE: 90\nthreshold_ARCH: 80\nthreshold_IMPL: 50\ntier_HIGH: 90\ntier_MED_HIGH: 70\ntier_MED: 50\ntier_MED_LOW: 30\ntier_MUST_GRILL: 0\nEOF\n}\n\nclean_state() {\n  rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR/agent-workspace\"\n  stage_weights\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  bash \"$HOOK_COPY\" </dev/null >/dev/null 2>&1\n}\n\n# --- TC1: score=95 → HIGH-CONFIDENCE tier ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\n2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\tLANGUAGE\tdecision_correctness\t0.5\tD-001\tsrc-1\tr1\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif [ ! -f \"$INDEX\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: $INDEX not produced\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC1: expected 'HIGH-CONFIDENCE' for score=95\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC1: score=95 → HIGH-CONFIDENCE\"\n\n# --- TC2: score=20 → MUST-GRILL tier ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nSCOPE\t20\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t3\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -q \"MUST-GRILL\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC2: expected 'MUST-GRILL' for score=20\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC2: score=20 → MUST-GRILL\"\n\n# --- TC3: 5 categories at 5 different tier levels ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nDOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS\t75\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nDESIGN_THINKING\t55\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nSCOPE\t35\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t2\nDECISION_ROUTING\t15\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t5\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\n# Each tier should appear once via distinct score-range markers\nfor tier_marker in \"HIGH-CONFIDENCE\" \"MED-HIGH\" \"0.50-0.69\" \"MED-LOW\" \"MUST-GRILL\"; do\n  if ! grep -qF \"$tier_marker\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL TC3: expected tier marker '$tier_marker' in output\"\n    cat \"$INDEX\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\n# All 5 categories should appear as table rows\nfor cat_name in LANGUAGE DOMAIN_UBIQUITOUS DESIGN_THINKING SCOPE DECISION_ROUTING; do\n  if ! grep -qF \"| $cat_name |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n    echo \"FAIL TC3: expected category $cat_name row\"\n    cat \"$INDEX\"\n    exit 1\n  fi\ndone\necho \"PASS TC3: 5 categories at 5 tier levels rendered\"\n\n# --- TC4: events.tsv with 12 entries → top-10 only ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t50\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\n{\n  printf \"ts\\tcategory\\tevent_type\\tdelta\\tdecision_id\\tsource_evidence\\treason\\n\"\n  # Generate 12 events deterministically (per L-S62-1: no `yes|head`; seq+printf instead)\n  for i in $(seq 1 12); do\n    printf \"2026-05-05T10:%02d:00Z\\tLANGUAGE\\tdecision_correctness\\t0.5\\tD-%03d\\tsrc-%d\\tevent-%02d\\n\" \"$i\" \"$i\" \"$i\" \"$i\"\n  done\n} > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\"\nrun_hook\nEVENT_COUNT=$(grep -c \"^| 2026-05-05T10:\" \"$INDEX\" || echo 0)\nif [ \"$EVENT_COUNT\" != \"10\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: expected 10 event rows (top-10); got $EVENT_COUNT\"\n  grep \"^| 2026-05-05T10:\" \"$INDEX\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\n# events 03-12 should appear (last 10 from tail -n 10), events 01-02 should NOT\nif grep -q \"event-01\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: oldest event-01 should be tail-cut\"\n  cat \"$INDEX\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"event-12\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC4: newest event-12 should be present\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC4: 12 events → top-10 rendered (oldest 2 cut)\"\n\n# --- TC5: thresholds rendered from weights.yaml ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t50\t5\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\nif ! grep -qF \"| CHARTER | 99 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| CHARTER | 99 |' threshold row\"\n  grep \"CHARTER\" \"$INDEX\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| SCOPE | 90 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| SCOPE | 90 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| ARCH | 80 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| ARCH | 80 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -qF \"| IMPL | 50 |\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC5: expected '| IMPL | 50 |' threshold row\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC5: thresholds rendered from weights.yaml\"\n\n# --- TC6: idempotent — 2nd run produces same _index.md modulo RENDER_TS ---\nclean_state\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/state.tsv\" <<'EOF'\ncategory\tcurrent_score\tsample_count\tlast_updated_ts\tmust_grill_remaining\nLANGUAGE\t95\t10\t2026-05-05T10:00:00Z\t0\nEOF\ncat > \"$SYNC_DIR/events.tsv\" <<'EOF'\nts\tcategory\tevent_type\tdelta\tdecision_id\tsource_evidence\treason\nEOF\nrun_hook\ncp \"$INDEX\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.md\"\nrun_hook\n# Filter out the variable RENDER_TS line (\"> **Last rendered**: ...\")\ngrep -v \"Last rendered\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.md\" > \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\"\ngrep -v \"Last rendered\" \"$INDEX\"           > \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\"\nif ! diff -q \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: idempotency violated outside RENDER_TS line\"\n  diff \"$TEMPDIR/.run1.filt\" \"$TEMPDIR/.run2.filt\" || true\n  exit 1\nfi\nif ! grep -q \"Confidence Score Index\" \"$INDEX\"; then\n  echo \"FAIL TC6: 2nd run output missing header\"\n  exit 1\nfi\necho \"PASS TC6: idempotent — 2nd run identical modulo RENDER_TS\"\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL FIRING-TESTS PASSED (6/6) ===\"\necho \"sync-tracker-render.sh externally-observable behavior verified.\"\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fbdf952e-b183-4273-9d20-88965926f9bf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d6a934c6-2410-424f-817b-82d8b866edc2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Implements L-S11-1 (Phase 0 hook portability), L-S13-1 (producer-consumer log\n5\t# path mismatch), and S16 success criterion #10 (drift signal D11 / D-IDENTITY extension\n6\t# scanning for fabricated SUPERVISED-mode regression in autonomous_mode=true ONLY-mode\n7\t# stockforge).\n8\t#\n9\t# Wires: Stop hook in .claude/settings.json. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n10\t# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + (if violations >0) one notification\n11\t# file in human-workspace/notifications/.\n12\t#\n13\t# Per Q-E2 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion frequency = phase-boundary; this hook detects\n14\t# drift continuously but does NOT auto-promote. Surface to agent who triages next turn.\n15\t# Per Q-E3 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion priority = hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST.\n16\t# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only. L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\n17\tset -uo pipefail\n18\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n19\t\n20\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n21\tHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\n22\tLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n23\tNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n24\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n25\t\n26\tmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n27\t\n28\tVIOLATIONS=0\n29\tVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n30\t\n31\temit() {\n32\t  local code=\"$1\" file=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n33\t  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n34\t  VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${code}: ${file} — ${detail}\"$'\\n'\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\t# === Check 1: L-S11-1 Phase 0 portability — no python/jq/yq/pip/npm in scripts/hooks ===\n38\t# Exempt: comments, strings, and explicit Phase-1+ stubs marked with \"# PHASE-1+\" guard comment.\n39\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n40\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n41\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n42\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n43\t    # Skip self.\n44\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n45\t    # Strip comments + heredocs roughly: scan only \"executable\" lines (not # comments).\n46\t    NON_COMMENT=\"$(grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*#' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n47\t    # Look for forbidden tooling invocations as command tokens.\n48\t    if printf '%s' \"$NON_COMMENT\" | grep -qE '(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])(python3?|jq|yq|pip|pip3|npm|pnpm)([[:space:]]|$)'; then\n49\t      emit \"L-S11-1-PORTABILITY\" \"$bn\" \"non-Phase-0-portable invocation (python/jq/yq/pip/npm) — Phase 0 doctrine = bash + POSIX only\"\n50\t    fi\n51\t  done\n52\tfi\n53\t\n54\t# === Check 4: L-S43b-9 printf format-string starting with `-` needs `--` sentinel ===\n55\t# `printf '-foo'` interprets `-foo` as an option flag, not as the format string. Fix is\n56\t# `printf -- '-foo'`. Encountered 2x during HR-3 deploy per L-S43b-9. False-positive-safe:\n57\t# matches only lines where printf is followed by a literal quoted dash, AND the same line\n58\t# does NOT also contain `printf --` (which means the sentinel is already present).\n59\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n60\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n61\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n62\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n63\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n64\t    BAD=\"$(grep -nE \"printf[[:space:]]+['\\\"]-\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"printf[[:space:]]+--\" || true)\"\n65\t    if [ -n \"$BAD\" ]; then\n66\t      emit \"L-S43b-9-PRINTF-DASH\" \"$bn\" \"printf format starts with '-' without '--' sentinel — use 'printf -- ...'\"\n67\t    fi\n68\t  done\n69\tfi\n70\t\n71\t# === Check 2: L-S13-1 producer-consumer log path mismatch (silent telemetry brick) ===\n72\t# Heuristic: declared LOG-like variable but never used as redirect target within same file.\n73\tif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n74\t  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n75\t    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n76\t    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n77\t    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n78\t    # Extract variable names matching *LOG*|*FILE*|*OUTPUT*|*PATH* pattern that get assigned a path-looking value.\n79\t    DECL_VARS=\"$(grep -oE '^[A-Z_]+(LOG|FILE|OUTPUT|PATH|DIR)[A-Z_]*=' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/=$//' | sort -u || true)\"\n80\t    for v in $DECL_VARS; do"}]},"uuid":"cc4a83c4-158a-4e7f-bbae-4eb0d06750ba","timestamp":"2026-05-05T16:35:53.598Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\bash-hook-lint.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# bash-hook-lint.sh — composite static-lint for scripts/hooks/*.sh (Phase 0 portability +\n# producer-consumer integrity) AND a fabricated-default drift scanner for stockforge LIVE\n# config. Soft-warn only (exit 0 always; never blocks).\n# Output: per-violation lines to .session-hooks.log + (if violations >0) one notification\n# file in human-workspace/notifications/.\n#\n# Per Q-E2 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion frequency = phase-boundary; this hook detects\n# drift continuously but does NOT auto-promote. Surface to agent who triages next turn.\n# Per Q-E3 (closed S15 Batch 1): promotion priority = hook FIRST, skill SECOND, charter LAST.\n# Phase 0 portability: bash + POSIX only. L-S10-1: if/then/fi only; split-local.\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nHOOKS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks\"\nLOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)\"\n\nmkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$LOG\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n\nVIOLATIONS=0\nVIOLATION_LIST=\"\"\n\nemit() {\n  local code=\"$1\" file=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  VIOLATIONS=$(( VIOLATIONS + 1 ))\n  VIOLATION_LIST=\"${VIOLATION_LIST}  - ${code}: ${file} — ${detail}\"$'\\n'\n}\n\n# === Check 1: L-S11-1 Phase 0 portability — no python/jq/yq/pip/npm in scripts/hooks ===\n# Exempt: comments, strings, and explicit Phase-1+ stubs marked with \"# PHASE-1+\" guard comment.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    # Skip self.\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Strip comments + heredocs roughly: scan only \"executable\" lines (not # comments).\n    NON_COMMENT=\"$(grep -vE '^[[:space:]]*#' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n    # Look for forbidden tooling invocations as command tokens.\n    if printf '%s' \"$NON_COMMENT\" | grep -qE '(^|[[:space:]`(|&;])(python3?|jq|yq|pip|pip3|npm|pnpm)([[:space:]]|$)'; then\n      emit \"L-S11-1-PORTABILITY\" \"$bn\" \"non-Phase-0-portable invocation (python/jq/yq/pip/npm) — Phase 0 doctrine = bash + POSIX only\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 4: L-S43b-9 printf format-string starting with `-` needs `--` sentinel ===\n# `printf '-foo'` interprets `-foo` as an option flag, not as the format string. Fix is\n# `printf -- '-foo'`. Encountered 2x during HR-3 deploy per L-S43b-9. False-positive-safe:\n# matches only lines where printf is followed by a literal quoted dash, AND the same line\n# does NOT also contain `printf --` (which means the sentinel is already present).\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    BAD=\"$(grep -nE \"printf[[:space:]]+['\\\"]-\" \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | grep -vE \"printf[[:space:]]+--\" || true)\"\n    if [ -n \"$BAD\" ]; then\n      emit \"L-S43b-9-PRINTF-DASH\" \"$bn\" \"printf format starts with '-' without '--' sentinel — use 'printf -- ...'\"\n    fi\n  done\nfi\n\n# === Check 2: L-S13-1 producer-consumer log path mismatch (silent telemetry brick) ===\n# Heuristic: declared LOG-like variable but never used as redirect target within same file.\nif [ -d \"$HOOKS_DIR\" ]; then\n  for f in \"$HOOKS_DIR\"/*.sh; do\n    [ ! -f \"$f\" ] && continue\n    bn=\"$(basename \"$f\")\"\n    [ \"$bn\" = \"bash-hook-lint.sh\" ] && continue\n    # Extract variable names matching *LOG*|*FILE*|*OUTPUT*|*PATH* pattern that get assigned a path-looking value.\n    DECL_VARS=\"$(grep -oE '^[A-Z_]+(LOG|FILE|OUTPUT|PATH|DIR)[A-Z_]*=' \"$f\" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/=$//' | sort -u || true)\"\n    for v in $DECL_VARS; do","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":298}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e7171e51-46ef-436a-9d73-86c8e77f2a2d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"d6a934c6-2410-424f-817b-82d8b866edc2","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
